Stability
---------
    ok  no assignment oscillated in the last run
    ok  all 754 ledger verdicts are obeyed

What to do
----------
  1. Withdraw those 8 assignments in IDEN2, or accept them in the classification: at present the screen and the fit disagree.
  2. Carry those 3 new assignments into IDEN2 so that the screen shows the fit that is actually being used.
  3. Re-identify those transitions in IDEN2: they have been moved to another line since IDEN2 last saw them.
  4. Run sync_IDEN2.py in this set: it writes each line the way [hfs] iden2_display says.
  5. Run sync_IDEN2.py in this set: dlv.dat holds the wavenumbers of another set, so every window drawn around a prediction is drawn in the wrong place.
  6. Run sync_IDEN2.py in this set: it writes the pipeline's uncertainty - the line list's, or the value in inflated_unc_lines.txt where that is larger - into dlv.dat.
  7. Enter in inflated_unc_lines.txt each wider uncertainty in dlv.dat that was set on purpose, so that classify_lines.py and LOPT use it too.  Do not run sync_IDEN2.py on the others until that is done: it would narrow every one of them to the pipeline's value.
  8. Run sync_IDEN2.py in this set: it writes the line list's wavenumber, and a wavelength consistent with it, back into every drifted row of dlv.dat.
  9. Re-run level_positions.py (and level_shifts.py) last: their reports are built on everything above, and a report made before the fixes will recommend things that have already been done.

  They are in the order the pipeline runs.  Doing a later
  one first only means doing it again after the earlier one.

6 error(s), 6 warning(s), 14 check(s) clean.
